# CF2 exception list (spec 0.3, CF2 and MG6; Linear N2), built from b6e64c1d.
#
# Each line is a site of the frozen engine (core paths and native component tree, FC6b) that holds
# knowledge refused by section 15.3: a physical role or the machinery behind it, a model, closure or
# equation of state, a named numerical method, scheme, limiter, interpolation, approximation,
# controller, field operator or boundary family, a route table keyed on such names, a primitive
# selected by its spelling, a physical constant, or a case identifier.
#
#     <path>:<line> <term> <issue> | <excerpt>
#
# <line> 0 means the term is in the file path. <issue> is the Linear issue that takes the site over
# (OWNER_RULES in cf2.py). test_cf2_architecture.py compares the sites with this list per
# (path, term): a new site fails, and so does a listed site that has gone. Remove the line of a site
# you remove, in the same PR, or run
#
#     python -m tests.python.architecture.frozen_engine.refresh_exceptions cf2
#
# which never raises a count. The list was written at b6e64c1d, then written again at the same
# commit with the terms that the early audit of N2 added on 29/10. G3 requires it to be empty
# (spec 0.3, section 1.5). The exit of Phase 1 removes the sites of the mesh route except those that
# G04, G05, G06, G10, G11 and N9 take over in Phase 2, and every site left names its Phase 2 issue
# (section 18.3, as amended on 29/10).
